lognormal: log(S_T/S0) ~ N(mu*h, sd*sqrt(h))\n    # P(S_T > strike) = P( log(S_T/S0) > log(strike/S0) )\n    if spot<=0: return None\n    thresh = math.log(strike/spot)\n    mean = mu_per_h * h\n    vol = sd_h * math.sqrt(h)\n    z = (thresh - mean) / vol\n    # P(X > thresh) = P( (X-mean)/vol > z ) = 1-Phi(z)\n    # Phi approx\n    def phi(z):\n        # Abramowitz\n        t = 1/(1+0.2316419*abs(z))\n        d = 0.3989422804*math.exp(-z*z/2)\n        p = d*t*(0.319381530 + t*(-0.356563782 + t*(1.781477937 + t*(-1.821255978 + t*1.330274429))))\n        return 1-p if z>0 else p\n    # 1-Phi(z) = Phi(-z)\n    return phi(-z)\n\nfor name, mu, sd_h in [\n    (\"zero drift 7d vol\", 0, sd),\n    (\"zero drift 48h vol\", 0, sd48),\n    (\"zero drift 24h vol\", 0, sd24),\n    (\"7d mean drift\", statistics.mean(rets), sd),\n    (\"48h mean drift\", statistics.mean(rets[-48:]), sd48),\n    (\"24h mean drift\", statistics.mean(rets[-24:]), sd24),\n]:\n    print(f\"P(up) {name}: {p_up(mu, sd_h, hours, px, ref):.3f}  mu/h={mu*100:.4f}%\")\n\nprint(\"--- last 30 hourly closes vs ref ---\")\nabove=0\nfor t,cl,o,h,l in closes[-30:]:\n    flag = \"ABOVE\" if cl>ref else \"BELOW\"\n    if cl>ref: above+=1\n    print(f\"{t.strftime('%m-%d %H:%M')} c={cl:.2f} {cl-ref:+.2f} {flag} rng={h-l:.1f}\")\nprint(\"above count last30\", above, \"/30\")\n\n# BTC\nbtc = get(\"https://api.binance.com/api/v3/ticker/24hr?symbol=BTCUSDT\")\nprint(\"BTC last\", btc[\"lastPrice\"], \"chg\", btc[\"priceChangePercent\"], \"high\", btc[\"highPrice\"], \"low\", btc[\"lowPrice\"])\n\n# 1m last 30 for micro\nm1 = get(\"https://api.binance.com/api/v3/klines?symbol=ETHUSDT&interval=1m&limit=30\")\nprint(\"--- last 30 1m ---\")\nfor c in m1[::3]:\n    t = datetime.fromtimestamp(c[0]/1000, timezone.utc).strftime(\"%H:%M\")\n    print(t, \"c\", c[4], \"v\", round(float(c[5]),2))\nprint(\"last\", m1[-1][4])\n\n# seasonality: for past days, move from 05:00 UTC to 16:00 UTC\nprint(\"--- seasonality 05:00 -> 16:00 UTC ---\")\nby_hour = {datetime.fromtimestamp(c[0]/1000, timezone.utc): float(c[4]) for c in kl}\n# group by date\nfrom collections import defaultdict\ndays = defaultdict(dict)\nfor t,cl,o,h,l in closes:\n    days[t.date()][t.hour] = cl\nfor d in sorted(days):\n    hmap = days[d]\n    if 5 in hmap and 16 in hmap:\n        a,b = hmap[5], hmap[16]\n        print(f\"{d} 05:00={a:.2f} 16:00={b:.2f} chg={(b/a-1)*100:+.2f}%  vs ref-style\")\nPY","description":"Estimate ETH probability vs noon strike"},"content":[],"locations":[]}

math.sqrt(h)\n    z = (thresh - mean) / vol\n    # P(X > thresh) = P( (X-mean)/vol > z ) = 1-Phi(z)\n    # Phi approx\n    def phi(z):\n        # Abramowitz\n        t = 1/(1+0.2316419*abs(z))\n        d = 0.3989422804*math.exp(-z*z/2)\n        p = d*t*(0.319381530 + t*(-0.356563782 + t*(1.781477937 + t*(-1.821255978 + t*1.330274429))))\n        return 1-p if z>0 else p\n    # 1-Phi(z) = Phi(-z)\n    return phi(-z)\n\nfor name, mu, sd_h in [\n    (\"zero drift 7d vol\", 0, sd),\n    (\"zero drift 48h vol\", 0, sd48),\n    (\"zero drift 24h vol\", 0, sd24),\n    (\"7d mean drift\", statistics.mean(rets), sd),\n    (\"48h mean drift\", statistics.mean(rets[-48:]), sd48),\n    (\"24h mean drift\", statistics.mean(rets[-24:]), sd24),\n]:\n    print(f\"P(up) {name}: {p_up(mu, sd_h, hours, px, ref):.3f}  mu/h={mu*100:.4f}%\")\n\nprint(\"--- last 30 hourly closes vs ref ---\")\nabove=0\nfor t,cl,o,h,l in closes[-30:]:\n    flag = \"ABOVE\" if cl>ref else \"BELOW\"\n    if cl>ref:
